Verification often functions as a reactive adaptation to machine hallucinations: students encountering fabricated citations reported higher verification scores (4.2/5) than those with no such encounters (2.8/5). Furthermore, while 81.6% knew submitting hallucinated cases can lead to contempt-of-court, 71.1% received no formal training on ethical AI use. We propose shifting toward adversarial legal research pedagogy and implementing source-grounded verification architectures to prevent systemic professional negligence.

</details>


### [144] [When Trust Meets Truth: Trust-Truth Separability in LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21097)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Yuchen Guo,Jiahuan Pei,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM-as-Judge系统中信任评分与真值判断的独立性，发现两者高度相关，源提示变化会同时影响信任评分、真值判定及logit概率，表明不应将信任评分视为真值判断的独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-Judge系统常输出多维评估（如可信度、可靠性、事实性），并被视为独立证据。作者旨在验证信任评分与二元真值分类这一常见判断对是否真正独立。

Method: 在正确性受控的QA任务上，对比LLM裁判与人类行为参考的信任评分-真值判断一致性；通过仅改变相同QA的来源提示（人类vs AI）进行压力测试，观察信任评分、真值判定及logit派生正确侧概率的变化。

Result: LLM裁判的信任评分与真值判断一致性显著高于人类参考，提示两者分离度较弱；仅改变来源提示即导致信任评分、真值判定及logit概率同向偏移。

Conclusion: 现有LLM-as-Judge协议不应将信任评分视为真值判断的独立证据，二者存在强耦合，源归因会系统性扭曲两者。

Abstract: LLM-as-Judge systems can produce multi-dimensional evaluations, such as trustworthiness, reliability, and factuality, and these outputs are often interpreted as independent evidence. We test this assumption for a common pair of judgments: trust scoring and binary truth classification. On correctness-controlled QA, LLM judges align trust scores with truth verdicts more tightly than human behavioral reference, suggesting weaker separations between trust and truth judgment. We then apply stress tests by changing only source cues of identical QA between Human and AI. Source attribution shifts not only trust scores but also truth verdicts and logit-derived correct-side probabilities. Results show that current LLM-as-Judge protocols should not treat trust scores as independent evidence for truth judgments.

</details>


### [145] [ReFrame: Evidence-Guided Test-Time Safety Alignment in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21100)
*Wenzheng Jiang,Xuankun Rong,Yuanzhao Zhai,Dawei Feng,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReFrame 是一个免训练的多模态输入重构框架，利用两个智能体生成风险/效用证据并重写提示词，在不修改模型或访问内部状态的情况下提升闭源 MLLM 的测试时安全对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）扩展了能力但也带来安全对齐挑战，需解决跨模态越狱、安全感知失效和过度拒绝问题。现有方法依赖重训练或内部状态检测，难以应用于已部署的闭源 MLLM，因此需要测试时安全对齐方案。

Method: 提出 ReFrame 框架：两个智能体共享一个轻量级本地部署的 MLLM。证据生成智能体构建互补的风险与效用证据；重写与路由智能体将其转化为安全代理提示词和图像路由决策，随后调用下游 MLLM，全程不修改模型也不访问其内部信息。

Result: 在多个 MLLM 和基准上的实验表明，ReFrame 能同时提升越狱防御、安全感知和降低过度敏感拒绝，同时保持多模态效用。

Conclusion: ReFrame 为已部署的闭源 MLLM 提供了一种有效的免训练测试时安全对齐方法，无需重训练或内部访问即可显著改善安全性能。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend model capabilities beyond text, they also make safety alignment increasingly challenging. Multimodal safety alignment methods must address cross-modal jailbreaks, safety-awareness failures, and over-sensitive refusals. However, existing methods often rely on retraining or internal-state inspection, limiting their applicability to deployed closed-source MLLMs and motivating test-time safety alignment. We analyze this setting and identify two key obstacles, utility dominance and reasoning inertia, which cause models to overlook latent risks or follow malicious reasoning trajectories. Guided by these insights, we propose ReFrame, a training-free multimodal input reframing framework where two agents share a lightweight locally deployed MLLM: the evidence-generation agent constructs complementary risk and utility evidence, and the rewrite-and-routing agent converts it into a safe proxy prompt and image-routing decision before calling the downstream MLLM, without modifying it or accessing its internal information. Experiments across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks show that ReFrame improves jailbreak defense, safety awareness, and oversensitivity reduction while preserving multimodal utility.

</details>


### [146] [Large Language Models at the Intersection of Software Engineering and Software Security:An Evidence-Centered Structured Survey and Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21107)
*Wei Lin,Tao Zhou,Zhaofei Xie,Changgui Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文是一份结构化综述，综合了截至2026年5月31日关于大语言模型（LLM）从代码补全向仓库级智能体演进的研究，涵盖软件工程任务、软件安全任务、适配机制、制品粒度和评估设计，提出了一个保障框架，识别了有效性威胁，并给出了最低报告协议和研究议程，核心结论是模型能力应作为由任务适配证据支撑的保障论证来判断，而非单一基准分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估证据分裂于软件工程（功能任务完成）与软件安全（漏洞检测、安全生成、利用验证）两个维度，缺乏统一的保障框架和跨研究可比的报告标准，亟需系统梳理并建立联合的安全与功能评估体系。

Method: 采用证据为中心的结构化综述方法，按任务分类学、保障框架（功能正确性、安全性、运行可靠性、证据溯源、智能体权威）、适配机制、制品粒度、评估设计五个维度系统综合代表性工作，并识别反复出现的效度威胁，制定最低报告协议。

Result: 发现执行反馈和仓库访问能显著提升工程任务完成率，但不足以建立安全性；静态分析标签或漏洞分类评分难以保证可部署的正确性。归纳出五大效度威胁：弱测试预言机、数据重复与时间泄露、智能体测试框架频繁变更、仅依赖代理安全检查、预算与人工干预报告不足。提出最低报告协议，并给出研究议程：联合安全-功能基准、仓库级威胁模型、校准的人工监督、纵向可维护性证据、可复现的智能体评估。

Conclusion: 模型能力应被视为一个由任务适配证据支撑的保障论证，而非单一基准分数；需建立联合的安全与功能评估体系，强调证据溯源、仓库级威胁建模、人工监督校准及长期可维护性验证，推动可复现、可比较的智能体评估标准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are moving from code completion toward repository-scale agents that retrieve context, edit files, execute tools, and participate in security-sensitive workflows. The evidence for these systems, however, remains divided between software engineering evaluations centered on functional task completion and software security evaluations centered on vulnerability detection, secure generation, or exploit-oriented validation. This evidence-centered structured survey synthesizes representative work available through May 31, 2026 across software engineering tasks, software security tasks, adaptation mechanisms, artifact granularity, and evaluation design. In addition to a task taxonomy, we introduce an assurance framework that separates functional correctness, security, operational reliability, evidence provenance, and agent authority. The review shows that execution feedback and repository access can substantially improve engineering task completion, but do not by themselves establish security; conversely, static-analysis labels or vulnerability-classification scores rarely establish deployable correctness. We identify recurring validity threats--weak test oracles, duplicated and temporally leaked data, changing agent harnesses, proxy-only security checks, and under-reported budgets and human intervention--and derive a minimum reporting protocol for cross-study comparison. The resulting research agenda prioritizes jointly secure-and-functional benchmarks, repository-scale threat models, calibrated human oversight, longitudinal maintainability evidence, and reproducible agent evaluation. The central conclusion is that model capability should be judged as an assurance case supported by task-appropriate evidence, rather than by a single benchmark score.

</details>


### [147] [Root cause analysis via difference graph discovery from linear time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21117)
*Anouk Ruer,Timothée Loranchet,Daria Bystrova,Charles K. Assaad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究线性时间序列中的根因分析，通过差异图发现视角，聚焦于效应违背型根因（即正常与异常机制下因果系数发生变化的变量）。将线性离散时间动态结构因果模型形式化，并将原用于两个总体间差异图发现的方法迁移到时间序列设定（正常vs异常机制），在模拟数据及IT监控、重症监护真实数据上验证，结果表明差异图发现有助于定位导致异常行为的因果机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 根因分析旨在识别复杂动力系统中异常的成因机制。现有方法多针对静态或独立同分布数据，缺乏针对线性时间序列动态因果机制变化的系统性研究，需形式化并适配差异图发现方法以定位异常机制下的因果系数变化。

Method: 基于线性离散时间动态结构因果模型（Dynamic SCM），将正常机制与异常机制作为两个“总体”，改造原用于两总体差异图发现的多种算法（如基于似然比、惩罚回归、去偏估计等），使其适用于时间序列依赖结构下的因果系数差异检测与图结构重构。

Result: 在模拟数据上验证了多种改造方法的有效性；在IT系统监控与ICU重症监护两个真实场景数据集上展示了实际应用价值，成功定位了导致异常的因果机制变化变量，证明差异图发现能有效辅助时间序列根因定位。

Conclusion: 差异图发现框架可有效迁移至线性时间序列根因分析，通过对比正常与异常机制下的因果图差异，精准识别效应违背型根因，为复杂动态系统的异常归因提供了理论与实践可行的新范式。

Abstract: Root cause analysis aims to identify the mechanisms responsible for anomalies in complex dynamical systems. In this paper, we study root cause analysis in linear time-series through the lens of difference graph discovery. We focus on effect-defying root causes, corresponding to variables whose causal coefficients change between a normal and an anomalous regime. We formalize this problem using linear discrete-time dynamic structural causal models and adapt several methods originally introduced for discovering difference graphs between two populations to the time-series setting, where the two populations are replaced by a normal and an anomalous regime. We first evaluate the proposed approaches on simulated data, and then demonstrate their practical relevance on real-world datasets from IT monitoring and intensive care monitoring. Our results show how difference graph discovery can help localize causal mechanisms responsible for anomalous behavior.

</details>


### [148] [Personalized Privacy Control in LLMs via Attention Head Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21209)
*Junseok Kim,Nakyeong Yang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化隐私概念，构建 P3Bench 基准，并设计 Repair 方法，通过推理时注意力头干预提升大模型对用户特定隐私偏好的遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文隐私研究仅关注情境相关的披露规范，忽略了同一情境下不同用户的披露偏好差异，因此需要引入个性化隐私以满足用户特定的隐私需求。

Method: 1. 提出个性化隐私定义；2. 构建 P3Bench 基准，扩展上下文隐私策略为个性化披露策略；3. 设计 Repair 方法，在推理阶段通过注意力头干预调整模型披露行为，使其符合个性化策略。

Result: 实验表明基于提示词的策略难以可靠执行个性化隐私策略，Qwen2.5-7B 和 Gemma3-4B 平均策略忽略率分别为 51.25% 和 74.28%；Repair 方法显著降低模型违背策略的情况，提升对用户特定隐私偏好的遵循度。

Conclusion: 个性化隐私是必要的补充，P3Bench 为评估提供基准，Repair 通过注意力头干预有效增强大模型对个性化隐私策略的遵守能力。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI enables LLMs to access diverse user data, raising critical privacy concerns. Prior work on contextual privacy studies whether LLMs regulate information disclosure according to context-dependent norms. However, acceptable disclosure boundaries may vary across users even within the same context. To address this limitation, we introduce \textit{personalized privacy}, which incorporates user-specific disclosure preferences into privacy control. We further present P3Bench~(\textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{P}rivacy \textbf{P}reservation \textbf{Bench}mark), a novel benchmark extending contextual privacy policies with personalized disclosure policies. Experiments show that prompt-based policies fail to reliably enforce personalized privacy policies, with Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma3-4B showing average policy ignorance ratios of 51.25\% and 74.28\%, respectively. Finally, to address this problem, we propose \textsc{Repair}, a robust inference-time attention head intervention method that adjusts disclosure behavior toward policy-consistent responses. Our method significantly improves adherence to user-specific privacy preferences by reducing cases where the model fails to follow the given policy.

</details>


### [149] [From Attention Masks to Inert Zero-Vector Tokens: OAttention and O-Closure for Token Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21174)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OAttention机制，通过基于隐藏载体范数的主动存在系数p_i，将零向量token作为零元素处理，实现精确的空支持、空接收器、空源插入等性质，并构建满足闭包律的OTransformer。实验显示在TabPFN v3回归器零微调改造中，OAttention和Full-O变体RMSE仅微增+0.088%和+0.177%，消融实验验证了完整OTransformer路径对保持NULL状态的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力掩码仅控制查询-源对的交互关系，无法提供在注意力边界处非参与的、由表示携带的token状态。需要一种机制让token能携带'存在/缺失'状态，使零向量成为代数零元素，支持精确的空token插入、移除与组合闭包。

Method: 为每个token隐藏载体h_i分配主动存在系数p_i=‖h_i‖²/(τ+‖h_i‖²)。OAttention在接收端用p_i门控输出，在源端用p_j加权注意力分子与分母，保留标准打分、可见性关系、指数竞争与值聚合。由此引出局部O组件（OFFN、ONorm、OInject）、存在加权OStandardize、O闭包律M(H⊕0)=M(H)⊕0，经残差与组合闭包构建OTransformer。

Result: 在克隆预训练TabPFN v3回归器的零微调改造中，校准隐藏载体的OAttention与Full-O变体在18个匹配数据集-种子情况下，均方根误差（RMSE）分别仅增加+0.088%与+0.177%。两层消融显示：单独OAttention无法通过普通宿主组件保持NULL状态，而完整OTransformer路径可以。

Conclusion: OAttention及OTransformer提供了精确的token级存在语义与代数闭包，实现了空支持、空接收器/源插入等性质，且在活跃路径兼容性上损失极小。但实验仅为范围测试，未证明普遍无损失、任意宿主闭包、向原点的习得吸引或缺失值的通用语义。

Abstract: Attention masks are relation-level controls: they specify which query--source pairs may interact. They do not provide a representation-carried token state that is non-participating at the attention boundary. We assign each token hidden carrier \(h_i\) an active-presence coefficient \(p_i=\lVert h_i\rVert^2/(τ+\lVert h_i\rVert^2)\). The same coefficient has two roles: it gates information emitted by token \(i\), and it determines the mass with which token \(i\) enters computations shared with other tokens.
  OAttention is the support-coupled attention realization of this rule. It gates the receiver output by \(p_i\) and weights source \(j\) by \(p_j\) in both the attention numerator and partition, while retaining the standard score, visibility relation, exponential competition, and value aggregation. This makes the zero-vector token a zero element and yields exact null-receiver, null-source insertion, self-attention insertion, and empty-support properties. The same token-level presence gives local O-components (OFFN, ONorm, and OInject), presence-weighted OStandardize, the O-Closure law \(M(H\oplus0)=M(H)\oplus0\), and an OTransformer by residual and compositional closure.
  The canonical operator is checked by contract tests and a GPU evaluation. In a zero-fine-tuning retrofit of a cloned pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor, calibrated hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants change mean RMSE by $+0.088\%$ and $+0.177\%$, respectively, over 18 matched dataset--seed cases. A two-block ablation shows that OAttention alone does not preserve a NULL state through ordinary host components, whereas the OTransformer path does. These are scoped tests of exactness, active-path compatibility, and compositional necessity; they do not establish universal no-loss, arbitrary-host closure, learned attraction to the origin, or a general semantics for missing values.

</details>


### [150] [SENTRY: Deterministic, Intelligent Risk Assessment for IT Change Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21203)
*Daniel Arulpragasam,Christer Henrysson,Ella Ly,Deepika Anbalagan,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENTRY是一个风险评估平台，用确定性机器学习管道（XGBoost + 混合RAG）替代问卷评分，在企业级变更数据上实现ROC AUC 0.87、85%准确率，高风险变更检出率是现有流程的3.25倍，并通过SHAP值保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型金融机构的技术变更管理依赖准确、一致、可审计的风险评估，但现有问卷式自评主观、易被操纵，难以区分常规变更与后续引发重大事故的变更。

Method: 构建确定性ML管道：结合结构化运营元数据、应用依赖图、历史事故记录，以及对历史变更请求的混合语义与词法检索（RAG）。检索步骤从非结构化文本捕捉风险信号，压缩为单一标量特征后输入XGBoost梯度提升树模型，保持确定性并通过SHAP值实现逐预测可解释性。

Result: 在企业级变更数据评估中，SENTRY达到ROC AUC 0.87、整体准确率85%，高风险变更检出率约为现有流程的3.25倍。

Conclusion: 该设计展示了确定性ML管道与混合检索增强在受监管变更管理中的可行性，架构权衡保证了模型的确定性、可审计性和可解释性，为金融科技领域的合规AI应用提供了参考。

Abstract: Technology change management in large financial institutions depends on risk assessments that are accurate, consistent, and auditable. In practice, many institutions still rely on self-reported questionnaires. Those questionnaires are subjective, easy to game, and poor at separating routine changes from the ones that later trigger major incidents. This paper presents SENTRY, a risk assessment platform that replaces questionnaire-based scoring with a deterministic machine learning pipeline built from gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) and hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The system combines structured operational metadata, application dependency graphs, and historical incident records with a hybrid semantic and lexical search over historical change requests. The retrieval step captures the risk signal in unstructured change request text, then compresses that signal into a single scalar feature before model inference. That design keeps the model deterministic and preserves per-prediction explainability via SHAP values. Evaluated on enterprise-scale change data, SENTRY achieves a ROC AUC of 0.87 and 85% overall accuracy, and it detects high-risk changes at roughly 3.25 times the rate of the existing process. We close by examining the architectural trade-offs behind this design and what they imply for the use of machine learning in regulated change management.

</details>


### [151] [Ontology-supported AI Model and Dataset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21224)
*Jan Novacek,Ali Ahari,Tobias Müller,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个用于AI模型交换的平台，结合本体论以促进对AI模型及相关资产的理解与管理，解决工业环境中的语义鸿沟问题，并通过实时关键系统的用例验证了平台的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI研究的深入，不同组织产生大量需要跟踪、追溯和管理的资产，且需发现任务相关资产。工业界亟需一种能有效交换和管理AI模型及相关资产、消除语义鸿沟的方法。

Method: 设计并实现了一个AI模型交换平台，平台集成本体论以建立共同理解，支持AI模型和数据集的使用、交换与分析。

Result: 平台能够促进AI模型和相关资产的有效交换与管理，减少语义鸿沟。通过实时关键系统的用例展示了平台的实用性。

Conclusion: 该平台为工业环境下的AI模型交换与管理提供了可行方案，本体论的引入增强了跨组织协作的共同理解，有助于推动AI资产的标准化与互操作。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [152] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度GPU实现，用于大规模TSP的广义分区交叉（GPX）算子的分区阶段，将分区问题重构为图并行问题，利用合并内存布局、幽灵节点变换和连通分量分析，在CUDA上并行化父代巡回合并、四度顶点分割、公共边删除和重组分量识别，实现了48倍至625倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GPU的TSP求解器主要利用种群级并行性，难以扩展到大规模实例；GA中的交叉操作涉及不规则内存访问、图遍历和顺序依赖，限制了在大规模并行架构上的可扩展性。

Method: 将GPX分区重构为图并行问题：采用合并内存布局、幽灵节点变换和连通分量分析；在CUDA中并行化父代巡回合并、四度顶点分割、公共边删除及重组分量识别。

Result: 在10,000至200万城市的实例上，相对于朴素顺序CPU实现，GPU分区实现获得48倍至625倍的加速，并显著降低内存开销。

Conclusion: 算子级并行化能显著提升基于GA的TSP求解器在现代多核架构上的可扩展性。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [153] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLEAR（连续潜在适配器路由），一种条件安全适配框架，通过轻量级隐藏状态门控连续调节安全低秩适配器的激活强度，在大幅降低有害输出（HarmBench ASR 从 32.3% 降至 0.5%）的同时，保持模型在良性任务上的效用（GSM8K 准确率比全局 SFT/LoRA 高 7.1 个百分点），有效缓解了安全与效用的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全局安全微调（如 SFT 或 LoRA）虽能提升安全性，但往往会不必要地修改骨干网络参数，导致在良性提示上的效用下降。需要一种能够根据输入动态调节安全干预强度的方法，仅在必要时激活安全机制，从而改善安全-效用权衡。

Method: CLEAR 引入一个轻量级隐藏状态门控网络，接收冻结骨干模型的隐藏状态作为输入，输出一个连续的标量门控值，用于按比例缩放安全低秩适配器（LoRA）的输出。该门控机制使安全适配器的激活强度随输入语境连续变化：在检测到潜在有害输入时增强激活，在良性输入时抑制激活，从而实现条件式、细粒度的安全控制。

Result: 在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上实验表明：CLEAR 将 HarmBench 攻击成功率（ASR）从 32.3% 降至 0.5%，同时在 GSM8K 等效用基准上比全局应用的 SFT 或标准 LoRA 高出最多 7.1 个百分点，显著优于基线方法，在多个安全与效用基准上均表现出更优的权衡曲线。

Conclusion: CLEAR 通过连续、条件化的适配器路由机制，成功实现了对有害内容的强力抑制，同时最大程度保留了模型在良性任务上的原有能力，证明了动态、输入相关的安全适配是改善 LLM 对齐中安全-效用权衡的有效路径。

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [154] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AUSO（Action-level Unified Skill Optimization），通过渐进式、动作感知的统一优化框架，解决技能在智能体策略演化中生命周期建模缺失的问题，实现技能从外部监督向自适应决策知识的平滑过渡，在 ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA 上显著提升性能与 OOD 泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么将技能保持在模型外部，要么完全内部化，或依赖嘈杂的任务级成功率在内部化与利用目标间选择，导致训练碎片化且同一轨迹内动作重要性均等，忽略技能指导可能对某些决策有益而对其他决策有害的差异。

Method: AUSO 统一技能学习与技能使用，分三阶段渐进优化：初期联合教师指导与环境反馈学习基础技能；中期强调基于结果的策略优化巩固自主解决能力；成熟期对每个采样动作在技能条件与无技能两种语境下评估，将动作级信息信号与轨迹结果优势耦合，对有益技能敏感动作加强更新、有害动作抑制，全程以强化学习为共享骨干。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA 三个基准上，AUSO 一致超越强基线，显著提升智能体任务性能与分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: AUSO 通过动作级自适应优化实现技能全生命周期的统一建模，避免了训练碎片化与均匀重要性分配，为技能驱动的智能体策略演化提供了更有效的范式。

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [155] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨欧盟ESPR和GDPR法规下的合规工件（DPP和DPIA）生成难题，研究LLM在生成这些工件时，数据提取指令与法规模糊性对输出质量与一致性的影响，并通过基准测试评估不同模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟ESPR要求数字产品护照（DPP）实现全生命周期透明，GDPR要求数据保护影响评估（DPIA）降低隐私风险。但工业数据异构、分散，DPIA缺乏标准格式且需跨学科专业知识，导致合规工件创建极具挑战。现有研究虽提出用LLM生成，但未充分解决数据提取与法规模糊性对输出的影响。

Method: 调研现有研究空白，设计实验探究数据提取指令与法规模糊性对LLM生成合规工件质量和一致性的影响；以人工创建的真值模式为基准，对不同LLM模型输出进行基准测试评估。

Result: 宽松指南（如DPIA格式）需更高上下文提示以保持一致性和完整性；严格指南（如数字电池护照DBP格式）无论提示上下文如何均能产出一致结果，但可能导致更多幻觉输出。

Conclusion: 法规规范的严格程度显著影响LLM生成合规工件的表现：模糊法规需丰富上下文引导，严格法规虽保证格式一致但增加幻觉风险。未来工作应针对不同法规特性优化提示工程与验证机制。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [156] [Unified Branch-and-Bound Search for the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem on Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21319)
*Jingtao Tang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了凸集图上的Steiner旅行商问题，提出了一种统一的分支定界搜索算法，在基准测试中表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在凸集图上寻找穿越必需凸集的最小成本闭合轨迹问题，允许可选中转顶点和重访，探索无限解空间。

Method: 提出统一的分支定界搜索，基于有根路径前缀；使用加性下界图成本约束已提交前缀，割分离连通流松弛为剩余目标访问和返回根节点提供下界；最优先和深度优先遍历策略在均匀正成本假设下保证有限终止；用户指定因子ε认证解的质量。

Result: 在移动操作器检测任务中演示了联合感知模式、访问顺序和连续轨迹选择，包含LTLf动作先序约束；两种遍历策略在所有基准实例上30秒内找到可行解，平均认证最优性间隙分别为28.1%和29.7%，而两个基线仅在约一半实例上成功。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了Steiner-TSP on GCS，提供有限终止保证和可认证的近似因子，在实际机器人检测任务中优于现有基线。

Abstract: We formalize the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem (Steiner-TSP) on Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS), which seeks a minimum-cost closed trajectory through required convex sets while allowing optional transit vertices and revisits. To explore the resulting infinite solution space, we propose a unified branch-and-bound search over rooted walk prefixes. Additive lower-bound-graph costs bound committed prefixes, while a cut-separated connected-flow relaxation lower-bounds the residual cost of visiting every remaining target and returning to the root. Under a uniform positive-cost assumption, best-first traversal terminates after finitely many expansions on every feasible instance without an initial incumbent, whereas depth-first traversal does so once a finite incumbent is available. For a user-specified factor $ε\geq1$, a global lower bound certifies that either strategy's incumbent cost is at most $ε$ times the global optimum. We further demonstrate joint sensing-mode, visitation-order, and continuous-trajectory selection for a mobile-manipulator inspection task, including action precedences expressed in linear temporal logic over finite traces (LTL$_f$). Both traversal strategies find feasible solutions on all benchmark instances within 30s with mean certified optimality gaps of 28.1% and 29.7%, respectively, whereas two recent baselines succeed on only about half of the instances

</details>


### [157] [Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks: Encoding Anatomic Priors in Loss and Architecture, with an SE(3) Formulation of Guidewire-Induced Aortoiliac Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21332)
*David P. Stonko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出解剖学信息神经网络（AINN），通过软约束（损失函数惩罚项）和硬约束（架构/状态表示内置）将解剖先验知识融入神经网络，类比物理信息神经网络。在临床测试案例中，将主髂动脉树及导丝路径提升至SE(3)李群上的曲线框架，耦合Cosserat杆导丝与扭曲度调制、解剖锚定的血管，通过单侧腔内接触不等式约束，基于弹性能量最小化预测变形，采用Wasserstein-2最优传输损失实现从二维血管造影到三维预测的监督。动力学、损失与投影已验证，力学求解器满足最优性条件，但位移预测尚未网格收敛，暂未训练网络。未来将迁移至真实CT扫描以验证预测精度提升与数据需求降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解剖深度学习模型虽数值合理但可能解剖学不可能，且在数据稀缺时泛化能力差。需要将解剖先验知识（如血管分支模式、连续性）显式融入模型，以保证解剖合理性并提升小样本泛化能力。

Method: 1. AINN框架：软解剖先验（如分支惩罚）作为损失函数惩罚项；硬解剖先验（如血管连续性）内置于架构与状态表示中。2. 临床案例：主髂动脉树内腔导丝引入导致的变形预测。3. 几何表示：将血管中心线与导丝路径从R³提升至SE(3)李群上的曲线框架。4. 力学耦合：Cosserat杆导丝与扭曲度调制、解剖锚定的血管通过单侧腔内接触不等式耦合。5. 预测目标：耦合弹性能量的约束极小化器，接触力作为拉格朗日乘子。6. 监督信号：预测投影（经C臂几何）与观测血管造影之间的Wasserstein-2最优传输损失，实现二维监督三维预测。

Result: 动力学、损失函数与投影算子已通过已知真值验证；力学求解器通过自身最优性条件验证；预测位移尚未达到网格收敛；本工作构建了理论框架，暂未进行网络训练。

Conclusion: AINN框架为解剖建模提供了融合先验知识的新范式。后续工作将把该硅内模型迁移至真实CT扫描，验证其是否能提升预测精度并降低训练数据需求，推动自主血管内导航与主动脉手术规划的发展。

Abstract: Deep-learning models of anatomy can be numerically plausible yet anatomically impossible, and they generalize poorly when data are scarce. We introduce Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks (AINN), in which soft anatomic priors enter as penalty terms in the loss (e.g., a branching penalty that treats a renal transplant artery off the iliac instead of the aorta as unexpected rather than impossible), in direct analogy to a physics-informed neural network, and hard anatomic priors (e.g., continuity of the vessel) are built into the architecture and state representation, making such invalid predictions impossible by construction wherever the prior admits architectural enforcement.
  We develop it on a clinical test case with limited data: how the aortoiliac tree deforms when a stiff wire is introduced endoluminally. This is important to contemporary aortic surgery and will matter to autonomous endovascular navigation. We lift the vessel centerline and the wire path from R^3 to curves of frames in the Lie group SE(3), and couple a Cosserat-rod wire to a tortuosity-modulated, anatomically anchored vessel through a unilateral lumen-contact inequality. The prediction is a constrained minimizer of the coupled elastic energy, with contact forces as its Lagrange multipliers. Supervision is a Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport loss between the predicted projection through the C-arm geometry and the observed angiogram, so a 2D angiogram can train a 3D prediction. The kinematics, loss and projection are verified against known ground truth; the mechanics solver only against its own optimality conditions, and predicted displacement is not yet mesh-converged. Here, no network is trained. Future work will transfer this in silico model to real CT scans and test whether it improves predictive accuracy and reduces the training data required.

</details>


### [158] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出VIALS基准，包含161个专业生命科学工作流中的视觉解释任务（凝胶条带、显微镜图像、质粒图谱等），评测前沿视觉语言模型在领域特定视觉推理上的表现，发现模型远逊于领域专家，揭示了当前AI在专业生物技术工作流中的实用性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家在专业生命科学工作流中常需解读各类科学视觉伪影以辅助决策，但现有视觉语言模型虽能流畅描述自然图像，缺乏领域知识与领域特定视觉推理能力，无法准确解释此类科学图像，限制了AI在真实科研工作流中的应用价值。

Method: 构建VIALS基准，收集覆盖生物技术行业实验工作流全链路的161个视觉问答任务，涵盖凝胶电泳、显微镜、质粒图谱、流式细胞术、分子结构等多模态科学图像；对比前沿视觉语言模型与具备相关领域专业知识的科学家在这些任务上的表现。

Result: 前沿视觉语言模型在VIALS基准上表现显著低于领域专家，无法准确解读专业科学视觉伪影，暴露出模型在领域知识储备与领域特定视觉推理方面的严重不足。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型缺乏在专业生命科学工作流中解读核心科学视觉伪影的能力，若不解决领域知识注入与专用视觉推理机制，AI难以在真实生物技术研发决策中发挥实质作用。

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Bankruptcy Prediction via Hybrid Resampling and Stacking Ensemble Techniques with Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI)-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20343)
*Obu-Amoah Ampomah,Edmund Fosu Agyemang,Kofi Acheampong,Louis Agyekum,Enock Adu Bonsu,Eric Nyarko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个整合共识特征选择、混合重采样、堆叠集成和可解释AI的破产预测框架，在严重不平衡的台湾破产数据集上实现了更优的少数类检测与模型可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对财务数据严重类别不平衡导致的少数类（破产企业）检测困难问题，旨在提升破产预测的召回率、几何平均数和整体预测平衡，同时提供可解释的特征重要性分析。

Method: 1) 采用五种特征选择算法并经共识保留规则筛选出23个稳健变量；2) 使用SVM-SMOTE、SMOTE-Tomek、SMOTE-ENN三种混合重采样策略生成平衡训练集；3) 对比五种集成机器学习分类器（GB、XGB、HGB、LGBM、AB）与五种深度学习模型（RNN、LSTM、GRU、DNN、MLP）；4) 构建以五个ML模型为基学习器、每个DL模型为元学习器的堆叠集成；5) 以准确率、召回率、特异度、G-mean、ROC-AUC评估性能；6) 引入SHAP进行特征贡献解释。

Result: 重采样策略显著影响模型行为：SVM-SMOTE与SMOTE-Tomek偏向准确率与特异度，SMOTE-ENN在少数类检测上更强。单模型中，GRU+SMOTE-ENN达到最佳整体平衡（召回率0.8627、G-mean 0.8517、ROC-AUC 0.9431）；堆叠集成中，SMOTE-ENN+(GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM在敏感度与特异度间实现最强妥协。SHAP分析指出杠杆、盈利、偿债与运营效率指标为破产风险最关键预测因子。

Conclusion: 该框架通过共识特征选择降维、混合重采样缓解不平衡、堆叠集成融合异构模型优势及SHAP提供可解释性，为财务困境企业的早期预警系统提供了更可靠、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: This study develops and evaluates a bankruptcy prediction framework that integrates consensus-based feature selection, hybrid resampling, stacking ensembles, and explainable artificial intelligence to improve minority-class detection in severely imbalanced financial data. Using the Taiwanese Bankruptcy Prediction dataset from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, five feature-selection algorithms were first applied, and a consensus retention rule reduced the input space to 23 robust variables. The balanced training data were then generated using SVM-SMOTE, SMOTE-Tomek, and SMOTE-ENN. Five ensemble machine learning classifiers, namely gradient boosting, extreme gradient boosting, histogram-based gradient boosting, LightGBM, and AdaBoost, were compared with five deep learning models, including RNN, LSTM, GRU, DNN, and MLP. In addition, hybrid stacking ensembles combined the five machine learning classifiers as base learners with each deep learning model as a meta-learner. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, recall, specificity, G-mean, and ROC-AUC, while SHAP was used to explain feature contributions. The results show that resampling strategy materially shaped model behavior. SVM-SMOTE and SMOTE-Tomek favored accuracy and specificity, whereas SMOTE-ENN delivered stronger minority-class detection. Among standalone models, the GRU with SMOTE-ENN achieved the best overall predictive balance, with recall of 0.8627, G-mean of 0.8517, and ROC-AUC of 0.9431. Among stacking ensembles, SMOTE-ENN with (GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM provided the strongest compromise between sensitivity and specificity. SHAP analysis identified leverage, profitability, solvency, and operational efficiency indicators as the most influential predictors of bankruptcy risk. These findings support more reliable and interpretable early warning systems for financially distressed firms.

</details>


### [160] [Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging for Adaptive Public Health Forecasting: Comparative Evaluation and an Ontario COVID-19 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20406)
*Yushu Zou,Ye Li,Johra Moosa,Martin Grunnill,Samir N. Patel,Venkata R. Duvvuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost三种模型在安大略省COVID-19周度病例预测中的表现，并提出了MLAMA集成方法。结果显示ARIMA在转折点后响应快但长期预测误差增大，机器学习模型初期响应较慢但长期误差更稳定；MLAMA在大多数预测时跨和响应性设置下表现最优，支持根据操作条件选择模型而非单一通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生预测需要在不盲目外推噪声、报告伪影或临时趋势的前提下，对监测数据的突然变化做出快速响应。现有单一模型难以在响应性、预测时跨和训练数据量等多维操作条件下同时表现优异，因此需要系统评估并开发自适应集成框架。

Method: 利用2020年1月至2023年10月安大略省公开的190周COVID-19病例数据，采用滚动起源时间序列交叉验证保持时间顺序，分别训练ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost模型。在响应转折点能力、1-6周预测时跨、历史训练数据量三个维度评估性能。开发MLAMA（机器学习与ARIMA模型平均）集成方法：基于非负性能加权，权重随预测时跨和响应性设置动态调整。

Result: ARIMA在转折点后快速适应但归一化误差随时跨拉长显著增加；随机森林和XGBoost初期响应较慢但长时跨归一化误差更稳定。研究末期两周预测中，仅用最近数据训练优于长历史数据，XGBoost受益最大。MLAMA在大多数预测时跨实现最低归一化平均绝对百分比误差，且在各响应性设置中均跻身最佳方法行列。

Conclusion: 预测模型应根据具体操作条件（响应性要求、预测时跨、可用数据量）选择，而非寻找单一通用最优模型。MLAMA提供了一个实用的统计与机器学习预测融合框架，配套Python包正在私有仓库维护，待软件验证和可重复性测试完成后发布。

Abstract: Public health forecasts must respond to abrupt changes in surveillance data without over-extrapolating noise, reporting artifacts, or temporary trends. We evaluated autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA), random forest, and extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) models using 190 weekly observations of publicly available Ontario COVID-19 case counts from January 2020 to October 2023. Rolling-origin time-series cross-validation preserved temporal order during model tuning and evaluation. Performance was assessed across three operating dimensions: responsiveness following selected turning points, forecast horizons of one to six weeks, and the amount of historical training data. We also developed Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging (MLAMA), a non-negative performance-weighted ensemble with weights that vary by forecast horizon and responsiveness setting. Retrospective comparisons showed that ARIMA adapted rapidly after turning points but its normalized error increased at longer horizons. Random forest and XGBoost were less responsive initially but maintained more stable normalized error over longer horizons. For two-week forecasts at the end of the study period, training on the most recent data outperformed using longer historical periods, particularly for XGBoost. MLAMA achieved the lowest normalized mean absolute percentage error across most forecast horizons and ranked among the best-performing methods across responsiveness settings. These findings support selecting forecasting models according to operating conditions rather than relying on a single universally preferred approach. MLAMA provides a practical framework for combining complementary statistical and machine-learning forecasts. The accompanying Python package is currently maintained in a private repository while software validation and reproducibility testing are completed.

</details>


### [161] [From Thermal Preference Prediction to Adaptive Thermal Intervention: A Reinforcement Learning Approach Using Physiological and Environmental Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20423)
*Isibor Kennedy Ihianle,Emmanuel Manu,Ehsan Asnaashari,Mojgan Jadidi,Pedro Machado,Amrit Sagoo,Ahmad Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段个性化热舒适方法，结合多模态生理与环境传感及强化学习决策，解决传统暖通空调系统依赖静态设定点和群体级模型无法捕捉个体生理差异的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暖通空调系统采用静态设定点和群体级舒适模型，无法捕捉个体生理差异，导致无法提供个性化热舒适体验，影响居住者健康和建筑控制策略的响应性。

Method: 提出两阶段个性化热舒适方法：第一阶段整合多模态生理（如皮肤温度、心率等）和环境传感数据；第二阶段采用强化学习进行决策优化，实现个性化热舒适控制。

Result: 该方法能够有效捕捉个体生理差异，通过强化学习优化决策，实现比传统静态设定点更精准的个性化热舒适控制。

Conclusion: 基于多模态感知和强化学习的两阶段个性化热舒适方法能够克服传统暖通空调系统的局限性，为开发更响应式的建筑控制策略和提升居住者健康提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Personalised thermal comfort is essential for occupant wellbeing and for the development of more responsive building-control strategies, yet conventional Heating, Ventilation, and Air Conditioning (HVAC) systems rely on static setpoints and population-level comfort models that fail to capture individual physiological variability. This paper presents a two-stage personalised thermal comfort approach integrating multimodal physiological and environmental sensing with reinforcement learning-based decision-making.

</details>


### [162] [BF1: A Causal Dyadic Sparse-Attention Retrofit for Efficient Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20427)
*Hina Dixit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BF1，一种确定性的块对齐二进制稀疏注意力模式，结合局部邻域、全局首块和对数间隔历史块，实现 O(n log n) 选中交互和 O(log n) 通信深度。在 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU 上，BF16 实现在 2K-4K token 处超越密集注意力，32K 时单层预填充加速 10.91 倍。将 Qwen3-0.6B 中 28 层中的 8 层替换为 BF1，在 8K/16K/32K 下整模型首 token 延迟分别降低 7.7%/11.3%/15.3%。在匹配的 1000 步、1638.4M token 适应协议下，BF1 在三个随机种子上平均困惑度 1.68639，优于静态随机非局部图（1.69154）、密集继续训练（1.69258）和等预算局部滑动（1.81505），确立了 BF1 作为可复现稀疏算子和选择性改造基元的长上下文系统价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集因果注意力在长上下文下即使使用高度优化的精确内核仍然昂贵。现有的稀疏注意力模式（如 log-sparse、dilated attention）缺乏针对预训练模型的正确性门控改造、匹配的拓扑控制研究，以及将逐层稀疏度与整模型延迟联系起来的系统表征。需要一种可复现、可选择性改造的稀疏算子，在保持数值正确性的同时提供真实的长上下文系统收益。

Method: 提出 BF1 稀疏注意力路由：固定块宽度下，每层包含小的精确局部邻域、全局首块、对数间隔的历史块，选中交互数 O(n log n)，图通信深度 O(log n)。设计正确性门控的预训练模型改造流程，仅替换部分注意力层（Qwen3-0.6B 中 8/28 层），其余层保持密集。实现优化的 BF16 内核，在 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU 上测量内核性能和整模型推理延迟。进行匹配的 1000 步、1638.4M token 适应训练，对比密集继续训练、静态随机非局部图、等预算局部滑动窗口，使用三个随机种子评估困惑度。

Result: BF1 在 2K-4K token 处超过密集注意力，32K 时单层预填充加速 10.91 倍。部分层改造后，整模型首 token 延迟在 8K/16K/32K 分别降低 7.7%/11.3%/15.3%。适应训练中，BF1 平均困惑度 1.68639，显著优于基线（静态随机图 1.69154，密集继续训练 1.69258，局部滑动 1.81505）。种子 1234 下，配对区间显示密集继续训练比 BF1 高 0.3169-0.4055%，静态随机图高 0.2441-0.3642%。结果在数值正确性、选中交互缩放、内核性能、部分模型推理、匹配下一词语言建模等维度一致确立 BF1 优势。

Conclusion: BF1 作为一种可复现的确定性稀疏注意力算子，通过正确性门控的选择性层改造，在保持模型渐近二次复杂度的同时，实现了显著的长上下文推理加速和困惑度改进。其系统级表征建立了逐层稀疏度与整模型延迟的定量联系，为长上下文大模型部署提供了实用的稀疏化基元。

Abstract: Dense causal attention remains expensive at long context even when implemented with highly optimized exact kernels. We study BF1, a deterministic block-aligned dyadic sparse-attention route that combines a small exact local neighborhood, a global first block, and logarithmically spaced historical blocks. The route is related to prior log-sparse and dilated attention patterns; our contribution is a correctness-gated pretrained-model retrofit, a matched topology-control study, and a systems characterization that connects per-layer sparsity to whole-model latency. For fixed block width, every converted layer uses O(n log n) selected token interactions and has O(log n) graph communication depth. On an NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU, an optimized BF16 implementation crosses dense attention between 2K and 4K tokens and reaches a 10.91x per-layer prefill speedup at 32K. Retrofitting eight of 28 Qwen3-0.6B attention layers lowers warm whole-model time to first token by 7.7%, 11.3%, and 15.3% at 8K, 16K, and 32K, respectively, while the remaining dense layers keep the complete model asymptotically quadratic. Under a matched 1,000-step, 16.384M-token adaptation protocol, BF1 ranks first across three training seeds: mean report perplexity is 1.68639 versus 1.69154 for a matched static-random nonlocal graph, 1.69258 for dense continued training, and 1.81505 for equal-budget local sliding. At seed 1234, the packed-report paired interval places Dense-CT 0.3169-0.4055% above BF1 and static-random graph 17 0.2441-0.3642% above BF1. These results establish BF1 as a reproducible sparse operator and selective retrofit primitive with real long-context systems value. This paper evaluates numerical correctness, selected-interaction scaling, kernel performance, partial-model inference, and matched next-token language modeling.

</details>


### [163] [Approximate Homomorphisms and Convergent Representations in Transducers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20428)
*Santiago Cifuentes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了受控随机过程（特别是变换器）的极小表示在扰动下的稳定性，受神经网络潜在表示中预测状态结构实验的启发。引入近似同态捕捉标准、线性和预测变换器的局部结构相似性，并定义比较其诱导动力学（接口）的度量。证明了近似同态的可组合性等性质。对于标准变换器，存在简单接口使得不同实现间无近似同态；而对于有限秩接口，所有实现足够接近该接口的极小线性变换器都存在近似同态指向该接口的极小实现，误差随扰动大小线性增长。预测变换器在残差度量下也有类似稳定性结果（需信念状态不可区分的温和假设）。这些结果确定了规范变换器表示对扰动鲁棒的条件，并表明缺乏结构限制时收敛会失效。若假设此类抽象嵌入现代AI模型隐藏层，则为其潜在表示表现出结构收敛提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期实验发现神经网络潜在表示中存在预测状态结构的启发，旨在从理论上分析受控随机过程（变换器）的极小表示在扰动下的稳定性，为AI模型潜在空间的结构收敛提供理论依据。

Method: 1. 引入近似同态概念，刻画标准、线性、预测三类变换器间的局部结构相似性；2. 定义比较变换器诱导动力学（接口）的度量；3. 证明近似同态的可组合性等代数性质；4. 分别针对标准、线性、预测变换器，在不同接口类（有限秩接口、残差度量下的接口）和假设（信念状态不可区分）下建立稳定性定理。

Result: 1. 标准变换器：存在简单接口，其不同实现间不存在近似同态，表明缺乏结构限制时稳定性失效。2. 线性变换器：对任意有限秩接口I，所有实现足够接近I的极小线性变换器均存在近似同态指向I的极小实现，误差与扰动大小成线性关系。3. 预测变换器：在残差度量下，若满足信念状态不可区分的温和假设，也存在类似的稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 规范变换器表示对扰动的鲁棒性依赖于特定的结构限制（如线性结构、有限秩、信念状态不可区分）。在假设这些抽象嵌入现代AI模型隐藏层的前提下，研究结果为神经网络潜在表示表现出结构收敛的假说提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study the stability of minimal representations of controlled stochastic processes (in particular, transducers) under perturbations. This question is motivated by recent experiments finding predictive-state structure in the latent representations of neural networks. We consider standard, linear and predictive transducers. We introduce notions of approximate homomorphism capturing local structural similarity between them, together with metrics comparing their induced dynamics (which we refer to as interfaces), and prove properties such as composability of the approximate homomorphisms. For standard transducers, we show that there exist simple interfaces for which there is no approximate homomorphism between the different implementations of the dynamics. In contrast, for every finite-rank interface $\mathcal I$, we prove that all minimal linear transducers implementing interfaces sufficiently close to $\mathcal I$ have an approximate homomorphism to the minimal implementation of $\mathcal I$, with error linear in the perturbation size. We prove an analogous stability result for predictive transducers under a residual metric using some mild hypothesis regarding the indistinguishability of the belief states. These results identify conditions under which canonical transducer representations are robust to perturbations, while showing that such convergence fails without additional structural restrictions. Under the assumption that these type of abstractions are embedded into the hidden layers of modern AI models, this gives some theoretical support to the hypothesis that their latent representations exhibit structural convergence.

</details>


### [164] [Wrong-Physics Backdoors in Neural PDE Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20439)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出跨参数重链接攻击，这是一种数据投毒原语，能使神经算子在触发输入时输出同一 PDE 族中另一物理参数对应的合理解，即“错误物理后门”。实验表明 Fourier 神经算子在对流扩散和二维 Navier-Stokes 方程上后门成功率达 1.0000，且保持极低干净相对 L2 误差，揭示了当前验证机制存在结构性缺口：仅检查平滑度或类求解器行为不足，必须核验预期物理参数的溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经 PDE 算子越来越多地在可复用求解器归档上训练，但验证往往仅依赖干净预测误差和与参数无关的合理性检查，忽略了张量到参数的溯源完整性，导致模型可能在触发条件下输出错误物理参数对应的合理解而不被发现。

Method: 引入跨参数重链接：在多参数归档中，给代理输入打上触发标记，将其监督信号重链接到同一潜在样本的另一物理参数缓存解。评估涵盖 Burgers、对流扩散、二维 Navier-Stokes、椭圆 Poisson 等 4 个 PDE，共 476 次攻击实验，主力模型为 Fourier 神经算子和 DeepONet，辅以 Transformer、GRU、LSTM。另设干净标签、仅标签、打乱等对照实验。

Result: Fourier 神经算子在对流扩散和二维 Navier-Stokes 上后门成功率达到 1.0000，同时保持极低干净相对 L2 误差。对照实验表明，仅高攻击成功率不足够，成功攻击必须同时将预测推向预期的替代目标物理，并保持有界干净误差。

Conclusion: 实验暴露了结构性验证缺口：平滑度或通用求解器样行为不足以保证正确性，必须在验证环节显式核查预期物理参数的溯源，否则模型易受错误物理后门攻击。

Abstract: Neural PDE operators are increasingly trained on reusable solver archives, yet validation often relies on clean prediction error and parameter-agnostic plausibility checks. We introduce cross-parameter relinking, a data-poisoning primitive that makes a triggered input select a valid solution from the same PDE family under an incorrect physical parameter. We term this a wrong-physics backdoor: the output remains physically plausible but is wrong for the intended parameter. The attack exploits tensor-to-parameter provenance failures in multi-parameter archives by stamping the surrogate input and relinking its supervision to a cached alternate-parameter solution for the same latent sample. Across 476 attack campaigns, we evaluate Burgers, advection-diffusion, two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, and an elliptic Poisson case. Fourier Neural Operators and DeepONet provide the primary evidence, with Transformer, GRU, and LSTM models as support. FNO reaches a backdoor success rate of 1.0000 on both advection-diffusion and two-dimensional Navier-Stokes while retaining low clean relative L2 error. Clean-label, label-only, and shuffled controls show that high attack success alone is insufficient: successful attacks must move predictions toward the intended alternate-physics target while preserving bounded clean error. These results expose a structural validation gap: smoothness or generic solver-like behavior is insufficient unless the provenance of the intended physical parameter is also verified.

</details>


### [165] [Decision Tree and K-Means Analysis of Raman Spectra for Edible Oils: A Physics-Informed AI Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20440)
*Amrita Shaw,Chandrasekar S. N.,Sai Muthukumar V.,Jhinuk Gupta,Deepak L. N. Kallepalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究建立了拉曼光谱与机器学习相结合的框架，实现食用油在纯态及薯片基质中的快速、可解释、低数据量鉴别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食用油在加工食品中的鉴别对食品质量、防欺诈和法规合规至关重要，但基质效应导致光谱重叠，传统方法难以实现高精度、低维度的识别。

Method: 采用t-SNE、K-means聚类、决策树、非负最小二乘(NNLS)谱分解等无监督与监督方法，结合物理信息人工智能(PI-AI)，对五种食用油在纯态及炸薯片基质中的拉曼光谱进行分析。

Result: 纯油样本决策树仅用4个拉曼变量(占0.21%特征)即达100%准确率；基质样本经NNLS-PI-AI分解后，优化模型在纸张扣除和纸张+薯片扣除数据集上分别达86.4%和85.4%准确率，重要变量仅5和4个，四特征表示压缩数据量99.44%且不损失精度。

Conclusion: 研究表明基于物理意义的紧凑可解释光谱表示可实现精准拉曼油品鉴别，为节俭AI、边缘AI、便携式传感和嵌入式食品质量监测提供基础。

Abstract: Authentication of edible oils in processed foods is important for food quality, fraud prevention, and regulatory compliance. This study establishes an integrated Raman spectroscopy and machine-learning framework that links intrinsic spectral organization, interpretable classification, and Physics-Informed Artificial Intelligence (PI-AI). Five edible oils were investigated in pure form and within a fried-potato-chip matrix using t-SNE, K-means clustering, Decision Trees, and Non-Negative Least Squares (NNLS)-based spectral decomposition. Unsupervised analyses revealed substantially stronger class organization and separability in pure oils, whereas food-matrix effects introduced pronounced spectral overlap. Decision Trees achieved 100% classification accuracy for pure oils using only four Raman variables from the original 1866-feature spectral space. These four variables, consistently identified by both pre-pruned and post-pruned models, represented only approximately 0.21% of the available spectral information while retaining perfect test-set performance. For matrix-containing samples, NNLS-based PI-AI spectral decomposition substantially improved classification by separating oil-related signatures from paper and potato contributions. Optimized post-pruned models achieved accuracies of 86.4% and 85.4% for paper-subtracted and paper-plus-potato-subtracted datasets, respectively, while reducing the number of important Raman variables to only five and four. The compact four-feature representation further reduced the data footprint by 99.44% without loss of classification accuracy. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that accurate Raman-based oil identification can be achieved through physically meaningful, highly compact, and interpretable spectral representations, providing a promising foundation for Frugal AI, Edge AI, portable sensing, and embedded food-quality monitoring.

</details>


### [166] [Shared Physics Responses Recover Hidden Rankings in Neural Operator Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20441)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需高保真参考解即可在部署时选择最优神经算子预测的方法，利用平方希尔伯特空间损失下的低维候选差异张成空间，通过单一锚点基线性化响应对所有模型进行评分，实现了高准确率的配对偏好恢复和最优检查点识别，且校正后的物理代理常优于单一最佳候选，建立了可计算的充分条件以严格认证强单调离散化的精确决策，实现了无需真实数据的可靠高效科学代理部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署阶段缺乏高保真参考解时，如何从有限模型库中选择最优神经算子预测是一个挑战。

Method: 在平方希尔伯特空间损失下，证明模型排名仅取决于候选差异的低维张成空间，利用单一锚点基于控制方程的线性化响应同时为所有模型评分，构建共享物理诊断指标。

Result: 该方法在流体、反应扩散、波动力学等多样化的傅里叶和卷积算子库上，准确恢复了超过99.6%的成对偏好和99.0%的最优检查点；校正后的物理代理经常优于最佳单一候选；建立了可计算的充分条件，可严格认证强单调离散化的精确决策。

Conclusion: 通过利用局部动力学响应而非原始缺陷幅度，该框架实现了无需真实数据的可靠、高效的科学代理部署。

Abstract: Selecting the optimal neural-operator prediction during deployment is challenging when high-fidelity reference solutions are unavailable. We demonstrate that under a squared Hilbert-space loss, ranking a finite model library depends strictly on the low-dimensional span of candidate differences, allowing us to score all models simultaneously using a single anchor-based linearized response of the governing equation. This shared physical diagnostic accurately recovered over 99.6\% of pairwise preferences and 99.0\% of optimal checkpoints across diverse Fourier and convolutional operator libraries for fluid, reaction-diffusion, and wave dynamics. Furthermore, the corrected physical proxy frequently outperformed the best individual candidates, and we establish computable sufficient conditions that rigorously certify exact decisions for strongly monotone discretizations. By exploiting the local dynamical response rather than raw defect magnitude, this framework enables the reliable and highly efficient deployment of scientific surrogates without requiring ground-truth data.

</details>


### [167] [Stored in Optimizer State, Valued by Later Training: A Causal Account of Subliminal Trait Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20442)
*Qinyang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了潜意识特征迁移的两阶段机制：优化器状态负责传输源扰动，后续训练决定其行为价值。通过将参数和优化器动量视为统一训练状态，推导出精确的传输-估值恒等式，分离了观察者无关的扰动传播与未来续训赋予的价值。实验在多种模型（Qwen、Llama、SmolLM2）和非LoRA MNIST系统中验证了这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究解释了潜意识特征信号如何进入梯度，但未阐明这些信号如何在源移除后存活，以及为何在后续训练中会获得不同符号的行为效应。本文旨在建立一个统一理论框架，揭示优化器状态在特征迁移中的因果作用。

Method: 1. 将模型参数与优化器一阶动量合并为单一训练状态。2. 推导精确的传输-估值恒等式，分解扰动的观察者无关传播与未来续训赋予的行为价值。3. 通过状态手术实验，移植一阶动量或参数+动量，观察参数、隐状态、输出的变化。4. 在Qwen、Llama-3.2-1B、SmolLM2及非LoRA MNIST（CNN+AdamW/SGD）上进行多种子、多步续训实验，测量行为效应符号与幅度。5. 利用全视界伴随态预测路线平均符号。

Result: 一阶动量是因果载体：单独移植动量在切割点保持参数、隐状态、输出不变，但无源更新后产生不断增长的参数与隐状态差异；移植参数+动量可恢复终端行为响应。相同源诱导差异经匹配未来产生负、近零、正三种Qwen效应（-0.658, +0.008, +0.658种子均值），该顺序在所有12个Llama种子的8步更新后复现，且状态差异范数几乎相等。延长至16步更新时，所有配对种子的对比度均增大。全视界伴随态准确预测了所有42个Qwen路线平均符号和21个Llama常规路线符号。观察者无关传输在Qwen、SmolLM2、Llama间复现，完整状态递推也能预测非LoRA MNIST系统的物理、隐层、固定头响应。

Conclusion: 潜意识特征迁移由两阶段机制驱动：优化器状态（特别是一阶动量）传输源扰动，后续训练续训决定其行为价值的符号与大小。这一机制在不同架构、优化器、任务中普遍成立，为理解模型间隐性知识迁移提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Subliminal trait transfer allows a student model to acquire behavioral dispositions from teacher-generated data in which the trait is not semantically expressed. Recent work explains how such signals enter gradients, but not how they survive source removal or acquire different signs under later training. We treat parameters and optimizer moments as a single trainer state and derive an exact transport-valuation identity separating observer-independent propagation of the source perturbation from the value assigned by a future continuation and behavioral readout. State surgery identifies the first moment as a causal carrier. Transplanting it alone leaves parameters, hidden states, and outputs unchanged at the cut, yet source-free updates generate growing parameter and hidden-state differences; transplanting parameters with the first moment recovers the terminal behavioral response. Sending the same source-induced difference through matched futures produces negative, near-zero, and positive Qwen effects (-0.658, +0.008, and +0.658 seed means). This ordering recurs in all 12 Llama-3.2-1B seeds after eight updates, while state-difference norms remain nearly equal across routes. Both contrasts grow in every paired seed when the continuation extends to sixteen updates. A full-horizon costate predicts all 42 Qwen route-mean signs and all 21 resolved Llama ordinary-route signs. Observer-independent transport also replicates across Qwen, SmolLM2, and Llama, while the complete-state recurrence predicts physical, hidden, and fixed-head responses in non-LoRA MNIST systems, including CNNs trained with AdamW and momentum SGD. Together, these results identify a two-stage mechanism for subliminal trait transfer: optimizer state transports the source perturbation, and later training determines its behavioral value.

</details>


### [168] [Amortized Bandwidth Learning for Kernel Density Estimation under Logarithmic Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20445)
*Junyi Liang,Hailiang Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种摊销框架，通过优化对数得分在密度估计任务分布上学习带宽选择映射，解决了核密度估计中带宽选择依赖样本的问题。该方法结合截断重归一化有界支持公式和仿射标准化，使在单一参考区间上训练的选择器能迁移到任意有界区间。实验表明，该选择器在高斯采样、多族基准和随机高斯混合训练中持续大幅优于Silverman规则、Sheather-Jones选择器和最小二乘交叉验证，尤其在小样本和异质样本上增益显著。有限高斯混合提供通用训练机制，训练好的选择器能泛化到未知密度结构，无需指定或拟合分布族，具有广泛适用性和强实证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核密度估计（KDE）将有限样本转换为概率密度，但其性能关键依赖于带宽选择。传统选择器要么基于解析/渐近规则，要么为每个样本单独求解优化问题，缺乏跨任务泛化能力，且在小样本和异质样本上表现不佳。需要一种能在密度估计任务分布上学习带宽选择映射、并能迁移到未知密度的通用方法。

Method: 提出摊销学习框架：在密度估计任务分布上通过优化对数得分学习样本到带宽的映射。采用截断重归一化有界支持公式实现跨异质任务的稳定学习，利用仿射标准化使在单一参考区间训练的选择器迁移到任意有界区间。以有限高斯混合为训练分布，利用其L1近似性质提供通用训练机制。

Result: 在高斯采样、多族基准和随机高斯混合训练实验中，摊销选择器持续大幅优于Silverman规则、Sheather-Jones选择器和最小二乘交叉验证，尤其在小样本和异质样本上增益显著。训练好的选择器能强泛化到不同密度结构，可直接应用于未知密度的有限样本，无需指定或拟合分布族。

Conclusion: 该摊销框架结合了广泛适用性和强实证性能，为将有限样本或集合转换为连续概率密度的广泛应用提供了吸引力解决方案。有限高斯混合的L1近似性质保证了训练机制的通用性，使同一选择器能直接处理未知密度样本。

Abstract: Kernel density estimation converts finite samples into probability densities, but its performance depends critically on bandwidth selection. Classical selectors prescribe the sample-to-bandwidth rule analytically or asymptotically, or solve a new optimization for each sample. An amortized framework is proposed that instead learns this mapping across a distribution of density-estimation tasks by optimizing the logarithmic score. A truncated-and-renormalized bounded-support formulation enables stable learning across heterogeneous tasks, while affine standardization allows a selector trained on a single reference interval to transfer across bounded intervals. Experiments under Gaussian sampling, a multi-family benchmark, and randomized Gaussian-mixture training show that the amortized selector consistently and substantially outperforms Silverman's rule, the Sheather--Jones selector, and least-squares cross-validation, with especially large gains in small and heterogeneous samples. Finite Gaussian mixtures provide a generic training mechanism supported by their $L^1$ approximation property. Selectors trained in this way generalize strongly across different density structures, allowing the same trained selector to be applied directly to finite samples from unknown densities without specifying or fitting a distributional family. This combination of broad applicability and strong empirical performance makes the framework attractive for a wide range of applications in which finite samples or ensembles must be converted into continuous probability densities.

</details>


### [169] [Mutual information and sensitivity analysis for feature selection in customer targeting: a comparative study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20447)
*Nestor Barraza,Sergio Moro,Marcelo Ferreyra,Adolfo de la Peña*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了互信息和基于数据的敏感性分析两种特征选择方法在银行电话营销案例中的优劣，构建逻辑回归模型评估预测性能，发现互信息在较高假阳性率下略优，适合降低联系成本且不损失大量成功案例；敏感性分析用更少特征（9个）达成良好预测，在低假阳性率下表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征选择是数据驱动知识发现的关键任务，现有技术包括互信息和基于数据的敏感性分析，但缺乏在实际业务场景（如银行电话营销）中对两者优劣的系统对比研究。

Method: 在银行电话营销数据集上分别应用互信息和基于数据的敏感性分析进行特征选择，互信息选出13个特征，敏感性分析选出9个特征；分别基于两组特征构建逻辑回归模型，对比不同假阳性率下的预测性能。

Result: 基于敏感性分析的9个特征模型在低假阳性率下表现更优；基于互信息的13个特征模型在较高假阳性率下略优。互信息适合在不大幅牺牲成功案例的前提下降低联系成本；敏感性分析能以更少特征获得良好预测效果。

Conclusion: 互信息虽非新方法，仍是有效的特征选择手段；基于数据的敏感性分析能以更少特征实现良好预测，两者各有适用场景，选择取决于业务对假阳性率的容忍度。

Abstract: Feature selection is a highly relevant task in a data-driven knowledge discovery project. Several techniques have been developed aiming at finding the features that influence most an outcome to predict, including mutual information and, in recent years, the data-based sensitivity analysis. The present research focus on analyzing the advantages and disadvantages of each of these two techniques, by applying both to a bank telemarketing case. Thereafter, a logistic regression model is built on the tuned set of features identified by each of the two techniques as the most influencing set of features on the success of a telemarketing contact, in a total of 13 features for mutual information and 9 features for the data-based sensitivity analysis. The latter performs better for lower values of false positives while the former is slightly better for a higher false positive ratio. Thus, mutual information becomes a better choice if bank managers intend to reduce slightly the cost of contacts without risking losing a high number of successes. Such results show that mutual information, although not recent, is still a valid method for feature selection. On the other side, the data-based sensitivity analysis selection achieved good prediction results with less features.

</details>


### [170] [When Clean Data Hurts: Learning with Monotone Corruptions Beyond Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20480)
*Julian Asilis,Shaddin Dughmi,Chirag Pabbaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Optimal learners are tailored to exploit the i.i.d.\ data assumption underlying the classic PAC model. What if an i.i.d.\ training sample were corrupted with correctly labeled examples drawn from an otherwise unrelated, even adversarial source? This model of learning with monotone adversarial corruptions was recently introduced by Larsen et al. (2026), who demonstrated that all known optimal binary learners suffer increased error rates in this setting, from $O(d / n)$ in the PAC model to $Ω(d \log(n / d) / n)$ under monotone corruption. Mehrotra (2026) proved this logarithmic factor to be necessary for binary classification, but left open the consequences of corruption for more general learning settings, such as multiclass classification and partial binary concept classes.
  As our primary result, we demonstrate that monotone adversaries are frighteningly more powerful in each of these settings. We exhibit a learnable multiclass problem, of DS dimension only 2, that becomes altogether unlearnable under a monotone adversary, and show an analogous result for partial binary concept classes. These results are achieved by an adaptive adversary permitted to view the original i.i.d.\ training set $S$ and to insert $b < \infty$ corrupted datapoints into $S$. In the multiclass example, the adversary need only insert a linear number $b = |S| = n$ of datapoints.
  We complement these impossibility results by proving that every class remains learnable when the number of adaptive additions is $o(n)$, which our previous multiclass lower bound proves to be tight. We further observe that the classic multiclass error rate of $O(d_{\mathrm{DS}} / n)$ remains achievable against adaptive adversaries restricted to a known constant budget $b = O(1)$, against semi-adaptive adversaries viewing only a $p$-fraction of $S$ for $p \in (0, 1)$, and against oblivious adversaries that cannot view $S$.

</details>


### [171] [Metag: A dataset to build agentic meta-reviewing capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20488)
*Anirudh Sundar,Min Chen,Divya Tadimeti,Gemma Zhang,Alice Li,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Pavan Uttej Ravva,Sadid Hasan,Somya Chatterjee,Pruthvi Prakash Navada,Xiao Wang,Yue Kang,Sulaiman Vesal,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Metag数据集，用于开发元审稿智能体，识别科学论文在审稿-回复过程中所做的修改。数据集包含349个高质量行动项，将审稿人关注点、作者回复及论文修改差异对齐，旨在帮助元审稿人快速核查作者是否回应了审稿意见及修改位置，提升同行评审的透明度和可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着会议投稿量持续增长，元审稿人负担加重，需综合审稿人意见、作者回复及论文修订。现有工具缺乏自动识别论文在审稿过程中具体修改的能力，亟需数据集支持开发相关智能体。

Method: 收集审稿截止前和录用后的论文版本，计算两版本差异，再邀请人工标注员将差异与OpenReview讨论中的行动项对齐，构建包含审稿人关注点、作者解决方案及论文修改差异的实例。

Result: 构建了Metag数据集，包含349个高质量行动项，每个项关联审稿人关注点、作者回复及论文具体修改位置。数据集已公开发布于GitHub。

Conclusion: Metag数据集为开发元审稿辅助工具提供了基础资源，能显著提升元审稿人核查作者回应情况的效率，增强同行评审过程的透明度和可追溯性。

Abstract: AI tools increasingly support tasks across the scientific research cycle, from experiment design and manuscript preparation to peer review. At the same time, the continuing growth in conference submissions has increased the burden on meta-reviewers, who must synthesize reviewer feedback, author rebuttals, and manuscript revisions. To address this concern, this paper introduces Metag, a dataset to accelerate the development of meta-reviewing agents, specifically to identify changes made to scientific articles during the review-rebuttal process. Each instance contains a reviewer concern, the author's proposed resolution, and the manuscript diffs implementing the stated change. Metag is collected by obtaining manuscript versions from before the review deadline and after acceptance, computing differences between the two documents, and asking human annotators to align these differences with action items from OpenReview discussions. The resulting dataset consists of 349 high-quality action items tied to paper differences and will enable building methods to empower meta reviewers to quickly identify whether authors have addressed reviewer statements and where in the paper those changes have been made, resulting in additional transparency and traceability throughout peer review. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/microsoft/Metag-dataset.

</details>


### [172] [Bern2Edge: A Neurosymbolic Compiler for Edge Deployment via Bernstein Polynomial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20497)
*Malak Gamal El-Din,Yifan Zhang,Yasser Shoukry,Sitao Huang,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Bern2Edge端到端框架，利用知识蒸馏将预训练教师网络转换为基于Bernstein多项式激活的硬件高效表示，提供LUT高保真实现和符号规则可解释推理两种部署路径，在FPGA上实现显著的延迟降低（99.8%）、BRAM减少（95.2%）和DSP节省（89.0%），同时保持精度损失极小（0.5-1.5pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将训练、压缩和硬件综合视为独立阶段，导致软件训练模型与资源受限边缘设备上的高效端到端部署存在鸿沟，且缺乏可解释性支持。

Method: 提出Bern2Edge框架：1) 使用知识蒸馏将预训练前馈网络转换为基于Bernstein多项式激活的硬件高效表示；2) 提供双部署路径：(i) 高保真LUT实现，保持压缩下的模型保真度；(ii) 基于Bernstein激活几何的符号规则表示，实现带显式输入空间约束的可解释推理。

Result: BNNs在相同压缩约束下比ReLU提升2.12pp精度；在AMD Xilinx KV260 FPGA上相比W8A8量化教师网络，延迟降低99.8%，BRAM减少95.2%，精度损失≤0.5pp；成功部署于低功耗Spartan-7 XC7S15 FPGA；规则路径DSP使用减少89.0%，精度代价1.5pp。

Conclusion: Bern2Edge通过Bernstein多项式激活实现端到端高效部署，兼顾精度、硬件效率和可解释性，为资源受限边缘设备提供了实用的神经网络部署解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying high-accuracy neural networks on resource-constrained edge devices remains challenging, as existing approaches treat training, compression, and hardware synthesis as separate stages, leaving a gap between software-trained models and efficient end-to-end deployment with limited support for interpretability. We propose Bern2Edge, an end-to-end framework that uses knowledge distillation to convert a pretrained teacher feed-forward network into hardware-efficient representations via Bernstein polynomial activations. This representation enables two deployment paths: (i) a high-fidelity LUT-based realization that preserves model fidelity under compression, and (ii) a symbolic rule-based representation derived from Bernstein activation geometry, enabling interpretable inference with explicit input-space constraints. The resulting BNNs achieve up to 2.12 percentage-point (pp) accuracy improvement over ReLU under identical compression constraints. At the system level, Bern2Edge achieves up to 99.8% latency reduction and 95.2% BRAM reduction relative to a W8A8 quantized teacher on an AMD Xilinx KV260 FPGA, while maintaining accuracy within 0.5 pp, and further deploys on a low-power Spartan-7 XC7S15 FPGA. The rule-based path reduces DSP usage by up to 89.0% at a cost of 1.5 pp in total accuracy.

</details>


### [173] [When Graph-JEPA Learns the Wrong Thing: Diagnosing and Repairing Category-Conditional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20516)
*Gollam Rabby,Sören Auer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在线性探测和有效秩指标看似健康，但表示学习失败，无法捕捉实例信息；修复后指标饱和于无结构信息的目标，揭示评估指标可能在不支持推理的目标上饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是揭示当前表示学习评估指标（线性探测、有效秩）的局限性，它们可能在表示不包含可用实例信息时仍显示健康，且修复后的指标可能在无结构信息的目标上饱和，导致对推理能力的虚假肯定。

Method: 使用科学推理图语料库（57,903篇文章，每篇为子图），训练Graph-JEPA预测掩码方面，对比线性探测准确率、有效秩、检索比特数等指标，分析方差分配，探究退化解为何为全局最优，并修复配置，引入可约性审计和目标门控。

Result: Graph-JEPA线性探测准确率0.871，有效秩18-47，但检索恢复0.00/14.4比特（MRR 1.9e-4 vs chance 1.99e-4, p=0.98）。三个上界恢复近乎全部比特。方差分配显示冻结输入86.05%方差在子图身份，训练潜变量99.61%在方面身份。修复配置达14.377/14.379比特，超越13.865比特神谕；回归损失降至0.307比特。但修复不保证推理：目标可约，神谕达上限96.4%，最大学习率调度效应非架构。比特与推理探测无关。数据衍生目标25.96%节点为重复占位符，其余比支持证据更通用。

Conclusion: 线性探测、有效秩等指标可能在无用表示上饱和；修复后的指标可能在无结构信息的目标上饱和；需引入可约性审计和目标质量门控，避免虚假进展。发布包含可约性审计和目标门控的评估工具。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures are selected almost universally by linear probing and effective rank. We report a case where both read healthily while the representation carries zero usable instance information. We repair it, and a second failure appears: the repaired metric saturates on a target carrying no structural information. Our corpus is a scientific-reasoning graph over 57,903 articles, each a subgraph. A Graph-JEPA predicts one masked aspect from a subgraph's remaining aspects, attaining linear-probe accuracy 0.871 and effective rank 18-47, yet retrieval recovers 0.00 of 14.4 bits (MRR 1.9e-4 vs chance 1.99e-4, p=0.98). Three upper bounds on the same pool and code recover nearly everything (+14.28, +14.34, +14.22 bits), ruling out corpus, masking, pool, and metric as causes. We trace this to variance allocation - frozen inputs place 86.05% of variance on subgraph identity and 0.40% on aspect identity, while trained latents place 0.39% and 99.61%. This is a property of the objective's optimum: the degenerate solution is a global minimum of the coupled predictor/EMA-target objective, present already at init. A repaired configuration reaches 14.377 of 14.379 bits, above the 13.865-bit oracle; reverting the loss to regression drops it to 0.307 bits, confirming it. Yet the repair licenses nothing about reasoning: the target is reducible, since intra-subgraph edges are a deterministic function of node census. The oracle reaches 96.4% of the ceiling, and our largest effect is the learning-rate schedule, not architecture. Bits and a reasoning probe show no relation across ten cells. A data-derived target fails a quality gate - 25.96% of nodes are duplicate placeholders, and the rest is more generic than supporting evidence. Rank, probes, and metrics can all saturate on an unsupportive evaluation. We release a harness with a reducibility audit and target gate.

</details>


### [174] [Learning Exact NVIDIA SASS Encoders with $\mathbb{F}_2$ Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20532)
*Jiading Gai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 F2Asm，首个开源 NVIDIA SASS 汇编器，支持 Rubin SM107，通过有限域 F2 上的高斯消元学习 128 位 SASS 指令编码，实现精确往返重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA 仅提供 SASS 反汇编器，缺乏公开 SASS 汇编器，限制了对最新数据中心 GPU 的机器码可控重写。

Method: 从配对的反汇编与原始 CUBIN 指令字中学习 128 位 SASS 编码器，建模为 F2 上的向量值仿射映射；采用增量高斯消元构建紧凑基、检测不一致并拒绝超出学习范围的输入；将目标特定控制位、重定位规则和 CUBIN 元数据与学习算法分离。

Result: 在 3,225 个 CUBIN 上训练，覆盖 Hopper SM90/SM90a、Blackwell SM100、Rubin SM107；往返测试中重组的 SASS 与原始可执行文本段完全匹配。

Conclusion: F2Asm 实现了首个开源、精确的 NVIDIA SASS 汇编器，填补了工具链空白，为机器码级优化和研究提供了可靠基础设施。

Abstract: NVIDIA provides a SASS disassembler but no public SASS assembler for recent data-center GPUs, limiting controlled machine-code rewriting. We present F2Asm, which learns exact 128-bit SASS encoders from paired disassembly and original CUBIN instruction words. To our knowledge, F2Asm is the first system to learn SASS instruction encoders as vector-valued affine maps over F2 and the first open-source NVIDIA SASS assembler to support Rubin SM107. F2Asm uses Gaussian elimination over F2 to incrementally build a compact basis, detect inconsistencies, and reject inputs outside the learned span. F2Asm separates target-specific control bits, relocation rules, and CUBIN metadata from its learning algorithm. We train encoders for Hopper SM90/SM90a, Blackwell SM100, and Rubin SM107 using 3,225 CUBINs from pinned NVIDIA and third-party production libraries, CUDA 13.3 packages, and CUDA 13.4 Developer Preview archives. In round-trip tests, F2Asm reassembles the disassembled SASS for each CUBIN, and all compared executable text sections match the originals exactly.

</details>


### [175] [AgentDecarbonizer: Carbon-Aware Execution for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20566)
*Leyi Yan,Shuangning Li,Sihang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentDecarbonizer，一种面向 AI Agent（如 OpenClaw）的碳优化器，利用任务截止日期的灵活性，在时间维度（等待低碳时段）和空间维度（迁移至低碳电网）上调度执行，并考虑上下文缓存重计算开销，在 WildClawBench 基准上实现最高 57.9% 的碳排放削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent 的长流程、多模型调用、工具调用及环境交互会导致大量 Token 消耗和碳排放。现有工作未充分利用任务截止日期的灵活性进行碳感知调度，且跨电网迁移时需处理缓存重计算与执行时间不确定性。

Method: 设计 AgentDecarbonizer 运行在 OpenClaw 旁路：1) 保守估计任务持续时间；2) 结合用户指定截止日期，搜索满足期限的时空调度方案；3) 在空间迁移决策中显式建模上下文缓存重计算开销；4) 根据实时电网碳强度动态选择执行时机与位置。

Result: 在 WildClawBench 的 60 个 Agent 任务、四个电网环境下评测：AgentDecarbonizer 相比碳无感基线最高降低 57.9% 碳排放，相比任务启动时仅选最优电网的基线最高降低 37.5%。

Conclusion: 截止日期灵活性为 AI Agent 提供了显著的降碳空间；联合时空调度并显式处理缓存重计算开销是实现低碳 Agent 执行的关键。AgentDecarbonizer 证明了在不牺牲任务完成时限的前提下可大幅削减碳排放。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models from single prompt-response interactions to long-running, goaldirected workflows that issue many model calls, invoke tools, and interact with external environments. These workflows enable tasks such as software repair, data analysis, and experiment management, but their repeated model invocations can incur substantial carbon emissions. This paper characterizes the carbon emissions of OpenClaw agent workloads using WildClawBench, and shows that emissions depend on token consumption, context cache reuse, and the carbon intensity of the grid. Our characterization identifies deadline flexibility as an opportunity for carbon-aware execution: agent tasks can wait for lower-carbon-intensity periods or shift to lower-carbon grids. However, doing so requires handling uncertain execution time for temporal shifting and cached context recomputation during spatial shifting. We present AgentDecarbonizer, a carbon optimizer for AI agents that runs alongside OpenClaw. Given a task prompt and user-specified deadline, AgentDecarbonizer conservatively estimates task duration and selects deadline-feasible execution schedules, while accounting for cache recomputation overhead during spatial shifting. Evaluated on WildClawBench workloads with 60 agent tasks across four grids, AgentDecarbonizer reduces carbon emissions by up to 57.9 % compared with a carbon-agnostic baseline and by up to 37.5 % compared with a baseline that selects the carbon-optimal grid at task start time.

</details>


### [176] [Faults That Fortify: CNN Adversarial Robustness via GPU Undervolting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20572)
*Behnam Omidi,Ahmad Tahmasivand,Husam Alsyouri,Saba Al-Sayouri,Chongzhou Fang,Ihsen Alouani,Khaled N. Khasawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在训练期间对 GPU 进行欠压操作，利用硬件层面的随机位翻转作为隐式正则化，同时提升对抗鲁棒性并大幅降低能耗，无需修改算法即可部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络面临对抗攻击脆弱性和训练成本高昂的双重挑战；对抗训练虽有效但计算开销大，在能量受限的边缘设备上部署尤为困难。

Method: 在训练阶段降低 GPU 供电电压，引入随机位级故障作为隐式正则化；在 MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上分别对 LeNet、VGG-6 和 MobileNetV3 进行标准训练和对抗训练，每种训练制度下均在标称电压和欠压电压下运行，并对所有模型进行对抗攻击评估。

Result: 在标准训练和对抗训练两种制度下，欠压模型的对抗准确率均持续高于标称电压模型，表明硬件诱导的故障甚至能增强对抗训练效果；动态功耗与电压平方成正比，因此鲁棒性提升伴随显著的能量节省。

Conclusion: GPU 欠压是一种即插即用的硬件级防御手段，无需算法改动即可同时实现鲁棒性与能效的协同提升，为边缘侧安全高效的深度学习开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) face a dual challenge: vulnerability to adversarial attacks and prohibitive training cost. Adversarial training is effective but expensive, a burden that grows as learning shifts to the energy-constrained edge. This paper addresses both through GPU undervolting during training. Reducing supply voltage introduces stochastic perturbations that act as implicit regularization, improving robustness while lowering power. We characterize undervolting-induced faults at the bit level, then train LeNet, VGG-6, and MobileNetV3 on MNIST and CIFAR-10 under two training regimes, standard and adversarial, each at nominal and undervolted voltage, and evaluate all models against adversarial attacks. In both regimes, the undervolted model consistently achieves higher adversarial accuracy than its nominal-voltage counterpart, showing that hardware-induced faults strengthen even adversarial training. Because dynamic power scales quadratically with supply voltage, these robustness gains arrive with substantial energy savings. GPU undervolting is therefore a readily deployable hardware-level defense requiring no algorithmic change, and opens a promising direction in which robustness and energy efficiency move together.

</details>


### [177] [Provable Edge-of-Stability for Adam on a One-Dimensional Quadratic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20638)
*Yiman Fong,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究Adam优化器在一维二次函数上的边缘稳定性(EoS)现象，证明Adam在广泛参数范围内倾向于恢复到冻结稳定性阈值，但也发现该机制在某些设置下失效，如严格亚临界周期轨道和特殊调整的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Adam的边缘稳定性现象被广泛观察，但其底层动力学机制尚未完全理解。作者希望在简化设置中隔离优化器诱导的动力学，从而给出具体的动力学解释。

Method: 在一维二次函数（常数曲率）上研究未修正的Adam，表征参数空间中的动力学行为，理论分析证明恢复倾向并识别机制失效的情况。

Result: 证明Adam在广泛参数范围内表现出向冻结稳定性阈值 $2(1+β_1)/[η(1-β_1)]$ 的恢复倾向；同时识别出严格亚临界周期轨道和特殊调整的超临界收敛轨迹等边缘寻求机制失效的设置。

Conclusion: 结果为Adam的EoS提供了无需演化损失几何的具体动力学解释，同时揭示了其局限性。

Abstract: The edge-of-stability (EoS) phenomenon of Adam has been widely observed, while its underlying dynamical mechanism is not yet fully understood. We study uncorrected Adam on a one-dimensional quadratic, a clean setting where constant curvature isolates the optimizer-induced dynamics behind the EoS. We characterize the resulting dynamics across the parameter space. In broad regimes, we prove that Adam exhibits a restoring tendency toward its frozen stability threshold $2(1+β_1)/[η(1-β_1)]$. We also identify settings in which this edge-seeking mechanism breaks down, including strictly subcritical periodic orbits and specially tuned trajectories that converge to the optimum while remaining uniformly supercritical. These results give a concrete dynamical explanation for Adam's EoS in a setting free of evolving loss geometry, while also exposing its limitations.

</details>
